# 🛣️ P10: Sectors (Proposed)

**Status:** 🟡 Draft · **Sprints:** S47 to S52 · **Dates:** 9 Oct 2028 to 19 Jan 2029 · **Tickets:** 70

> [!NOTE]
> P10 builds the twenty sector packs SC01 to SC20 as data on the capabilities of P3 to P9 (record types, rules, documents, screens and a seeded demo company per sector), because every business capability a sector needs exists by then and the roadmap's exit criterion for sectors is that each sector's example company runs its use cases end to end. The placement is (Proposed), waiting for the owner.

## <a id="goal"></a>🎯 Goal

At the end of P9 every business capability is built, but a construction firm, a salon or a council still has to configure SimpleERP by hand. P10 ships what each company type adds ([D114](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d114-sixteen-sectors.md), [D116](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d116-sectors-section.md)):

- **Twenty sector packs** in `packs/sectors/`, each installing its record types, fields, statuses, workflows, roles, documents and dashboards through F2 ([D113](../../../06-decisions/f02-no-code-configuration/d113-record-type-gallery.md)), installed and updated from the plugin store (UC-F14-02) and offered at the first run (UC-F21-03).
- **Every sector use case, business rule and entity** of `docs/specs/08-sectors/`, including the benchmark gaps of [D545](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d545-benchmark-gaps-sc01-construction.md) to [D564](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d564-benchmark-gaps-sc20-public-bodies.md) and the shared capabilities of [D457](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d457-gaps-and-improvements-round.md).
- **A seeded demo company per sector**, from its example company where one exists, so that every use case's acceptance criteria run as tests (pytest for the rules, Playwright for the screens, Bruno for the API).
- **One epic per sector**, E10.1 = SC01 to E10.20 = SC20, of three or four tickets each; the sectors are independent of each other, so up to twelve tickets run at once, one per sector.

**Decisions:** [D114](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d114-sixteen-sectors.md) twenty sectors, [D115](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d115-sector-interviews-after-f12.md) interviews after F12, [D116](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d116-sectors-section.md) the sectors section, [D113](../../../06-decisions/f02-no-code-configuration/d113-record-type-gallery.md) packs install through F2, [D457](../../../06-decisions/sectors/d457-gaps-and-improvements-round.md) shared capabilities, [D408](../../../06-decisions/technical/d408-performance-and-profiles.md) performance targets every use case cites.

**Technical design:** [repository layout](../../../../technical/architecture/repository-layout.md) (`packs/sectors/`), [configuration](../../../../technical/architecture/configuration.md), [plugin SDK](../../../../technical/interfaces/plugin-sdk.md), [data model](../../../../technical/data/data-model.md), [release and versioning](../../../../technical/delivery/release-and-versioning.md) (sector packs are versioned apart), [tests and traceability](../../../../technical/delivery/tests-and-traceability.md).

**Non-functional requirements:** [sector technical standards](../../../05-non-functional/sector-technical-standards.md), [performance](../../../05-non-functional/performance.md), [accessibility](../../../05-non-functional/accessibility.md), [privacy and GDPR](../../../05-non-functional/privacy-gdpr.md), [France](../../../05-non-functional/legal-france.md).

**Left for later:** the council website of SC20, which uses F18 Hosted websites and domains, built in P11; until then decisions are posted through the portals of F2.

## <a id="demo"></a>🎤 Demo

On a fresh install, the owner picks a sector at the first run (UC-F21-03), installs its pack from the store (UC-F14-02) and tries its demo company (UC-F21-13). Three examples, one per kind of work:

- **SC01 Construction:** a site is opened, quoted by lots (UC-SC01-01, UC-SC01-02), billed by progress with the retention held back (UC-SC01-06, BR-SC01-03) and accepted with reservations (UC-SC01-10).
- **SC17 Beauty and wellness:** a customer books a service with a deposit (UC-SC17-01), and the deposit is refunded when the customer cancels in time.
- **SC20 Public bodies:** the public accounting, procurement and citizen services of a council run on its demo data (FL-SC20-01 to FL-SC20-05).

## <a id="risks"></a>🎲 Risks

- **Large tickets.** The owner's cut of two to four tickets per sector gives five to nine use cases per ticket (size L); a ticket that does not fit is split before it is planned, inside the 99-ticket limit.
- **Missing capability.** A sector rule F2 cannot express as configuration (formulas, workflows, record rules) needs code in its business service; it is raised as a gap of the capability's epic, not coded in the pack.
- **Country rules still (Proposed).** Several sector rules wait for their France-pack rule (D545 to D564); the ticket uses the plain-words rule and marks it (Proposed) until `packs/countries/fr` has it.
- **No example company for SC17 to SC20.** Their demo companies are built from their use cases' acceptance criteria until example-company pages are written.
- **SC20 and F18.** The council website needs F18 (P11); SC20's acceptance in P10 leaves it out.
- **Placement (Proposed).** The owner may move sectors earlier (for example a pilot sector), which moves their tickets but not their content ([Q20](../../../07-questions/q20-which-roadmap-option-orders-v1.md)).
